- **Sign-off (fixed):** "Until the returning night." Shown when the GM ends a live session (M8; End session itself arrives in M5 without it).

**Glossary (one name per thing):** Table library (its link "Table library ›", ruled 2026-11-18, ⚑ 2 on #958) · Setting rules · Optional rules · Rules for this campaign · Homebrew · Party stash · Pack (a character's inventory screen) · At hand · Equipped · Stowed · Tiny · Scene · Log · Send · Reveal · Handouts · Expedition · Session · Full rest · Downtime · Archivist · Treasurer · Company · Charter · Fiat · Player proposes, GM approves · Wield · Attack sheet · At hand pop-up (the Player tab's short pop-up of a thing At hand) · Mode button (the pack's word: Quiver, Pouch, Grip or Stance; never "Mode") · Mode sheet · Way button (a way of moving on a destination row, stacked; never "mode button") · Destination row · Obstacle. Claude Code adds a new name here in the same PR as the component that introduces it.

A region is shown by its own name and kind, never "Region" (rg-3, 2026-11-12).

**How it's enforced:** every component in the inventory lists its text slots and pattern; the gallery shows real sample text; an automated check flags banned words, exclamation marks and Title Case in the app's own text files (never pack content or player notes).

**Still in force:** in the UI a pack is **"Setting rules"** ("From your setting: River Rules") and its choices **"Optional rules"**; voting screens show no explanatory text about open or secret ballots; **Archivist notes** use a handwritten script (e.g. Caveat) at a larger size, in an ink-blue that meets contrast in both themes.

## Words batch 1 (ruled 2026-10-26)

The app's own text for the M4b gate builds. `{braces}` are filled by the app; setting terms come from the pack.

**Guided creation: one line per step (Ogham).** {race} uses the pack's own term; a step the setting skips doesn't appear, nor does its line.

| Step | Instruction |
|---|---|
| Which way? | Pick the way your table builds a character. (cs-2, dec-10 A) |
| Scores | Six numbers say what your character is naturally good at. (the dice go in the step's Roll row) |
| {Race} | The people your character was born to. (cs-2, dec-10 A) |
| Class | Your trade as an adventurer. (Design, 2026-11-07) |
| Patron | Your patron sets or narrows your alignment. (cs-2; shown where the patron does) |
| Adjust | Trade points between scores, if your setting allows it. |
| Who are they? | A name the table will remember. (cs-31, dec-10 A) |
| HP and money | Roll hit points and starting gold, then enter both. |
| Gear | What you carry into your first adventure. (cs-2, dec-10 A) |
| First spells | The spells your character starts with. (cs-2) |
| Languages | The tongues your character speaks. Then, only as a fact, "{Score} {n} gives you one more." / "… {n} more." (cs-2; `languagesLine`) |
| Weapon skills | The weapons your character has trained with. (cs-2) |
| Details | None of these change the rules. (cs-2) |
| Review | None (cs-2, 2026-11-12; the Review step, below) |

**HP prompt** (the roll first, then what to add; "(at least 1)" and "to each die" only when the pack says so; the ability label is the pack's). Above the dice: "▲ 9 is above what 1d6 can roll. Roll again, or send it and the GM decides."

| Case | Line |
|---|---|
| Hit die, level 1 | The Roll row: "Roll 1d4 for hit points and enter it."; what the score adds is said once, in the sum under the stepper (cs-8, dec-10 D, picture 19-D3): untouched "Average. +1 for Grit 13: 3 hit points"; entered "3 rolled, +1 for Grit 13: 4 hit points" (the ability by its full name) |
| Hit die, higher level | "Roll 2d8 for hit points and enter it."; the sum "9 rolled, +2 for Grit 13 (1 a die): 11 hit points" |
| Negative modifier | The sum "1 rolled, −2 for Grit 5: 1 hit point, never below 1" |
| Zero modifier | The sum without the score's part: "2 rolled: 2 hit points" |
| Full HP at level 1 (campaign rule) | Level 1 starts full: 8, plus 1 for CON. |
| Setting's own rule (pack note) | Roll hit points as your setting says: {pack's short rule}. |
| Setting's own rule, no note | Roll hit points as your setting says, then enter the total. |
| No hit die, fixed HP | Your class starts with {N} hit points. |
| No hit die, nothing given | Enter the hit points your GM gives you. |

**ⓘ texts** (3 sentences or fewer; the literacy and alignment texts are fallbacks when the pack has none).

| Topic | Text |
|---|---|
| Companies (A14; until M8, ruled 2026-10-26) | A company is the party's shared charter: how treasure is split and how votes go. You can play without one; the GM turns companies on in Rules for this campaign. (The titles sentence returns in M8.) |
| Literacy (fallback when the pack has no `literacyNote`) | Literacy is whether your character can read and write. Some settings tie it to a score or a background. Ask your GM how yours works. |
| Alignment (fallback) | Alignment is your character's broad outlook, in your setting's own words. It can matter to some magic, items and places. Your GM decides what it means at your table. |
| Party's lowest | New characters start at the level of the lowest active character, worked out when they're made. Retired and dead characters don't count. The number shown updates as the party changes. |
| Player proposes, GM approves | The player picks a starting level while making the character. The GM sees it on the review and approves it or asks for another. |
| Fixed level | Every new character starts at the level set here. It's capped at each class's maximum, so a class that stops early starts at its own top level. |

**Toasts** (past tense, what moved, then one action; 6 seconds). Stacks use the pack's `unitName` (1) and `pluralName` (2+), otherwise "Name ×N". "away" marks a give that leaves the party.

| Action | Toast |
|---|---|
| Drop (single) | Dropped Lantern · Undo |
| Drop (some of a stack) | Dropped 3 torches · Undo |
| Drop all | Dropped all 6 torches · Undo |
| Give | Gave 3 rations to Ilsa · Undo |
| Give to someone outside the party | Gave 3 rations away to Odo · Undo |
| Empty a container | Emptied Backpack · 4 things dropped · Undo |
| Last of a stack | Rations: used the last one · Undo |
| HP from the damage pad (damage) | Took 3 damage · HP 6 · Undo |
| HP from the damage pad (heal) | Healed 2 · HP 8 · Undo |
| HP in place, a burst of − (Player screen, 2026-11-08) | Took 3 damage, 5 of 8 · Undo (one toast for the burst, updated as you tap) |
| HP in place, a burst of + | Healed 3, 8 of 8, at the most · Undo |
| HP in place, at 0 | Took 5 damage, 0 of 8: Down · Undo (": Down" only when the rules set's `death.at` says so; otherwise "0 of 8, the lowest it goes") |
| HP in place, a tap that changes nothing | Wren is at the most already, 8 of 8 (no Undo) · Wren is at the lowest already, 0 of 8 |
| Gear that stops casting (ruled) | Chainmail stops arcane casting · Undo |
| …when the pack blocks all casting | Chainmail stops your casting · Undo |
| Light burnt out | Torch burnt out · Light another (no Undo: undo the turn from the log) |

**Empty states (Ogham)**

| Where | Line | Action |
|---|---|---|
| No characters yet | No one is in the ledger yet. Make your first character. | Make a character |
| Empty pack | Nothing carried yet. Add what you take on the road. | Add item |
| Empty Party stash | Nothing in the Party stash yet. Things given to it show here. | (none) |
| Empty log | The log is blank. What happens at the table is kept here. | (none) |
| No setting rules (GM) | No setting rules yet. Add your setting to fill them in. | Add setting rules |
| No setting rules (player) | Your GM hasn't added setting rules yet. | (none) |

**The Resend pop-up** (a dialog: it asks for a choice). Title "{N} things still to do" ("1 thing still to do"); one line per open item in reading order ("Hit points: the GM asked you to roll again.", "Starting money: over by 3 gp."); body "You can resend now; the GM will see these marked."; buttons "Fix <first item>" (primary, jumps to its step) and "Resend anyway" (a small secondary, fl-1).

**Fight words** (fight dry run, ruled 2026-10-29; `{braces}` filled by the app)

| Where | Words |
|---|---|
| Encounter outcomes | Fight · Talk · Avoid |
| Ending a turn | End our turn (side initiative, players) · End foes' turn (GM) · End turn (individual initiative) · Next round (GM's override); renamed 2026-10-29 so the buttons start with a verb, with no exceptions (End foes' turn replaced Foes' turn done) |
| Ending a fight | Send loot to the party · Finish without loot (replaced Done without loot, 2026-10-29) |
| Delay | Delay (button) · Delayed (the flag) |
| Foe card | + Status · Note · Share (a Note) |
| A player's target on the GM's screen | {Name} → {foe} {N} |
| GM mirror | d20 +1 · hits {Name} on 14+ |
| Encounter detail chips (optional, after Talk or Avoid) | Bribed · Parleyed · Sneaked past · They left · Retreated · log "Talked" / "Avoided" when none is tapped |
| Down and the death panel | Down · Dies · Not yet · Helped · Healed · Stabilised · Survives |
| A spell timer waiting for its dice duration (GM console) | ? turns · Set |
| Bonus HP | Add bonus HP… (character's ⋯) · +{N} bonus · {N} from bonus HP · {N} from HP |
| Taking from a Down or dead character | Take from {name} · toast "Took {item} from {name} · Undo" |
| We run | We run · Got away · Caught · toast "We run · Undo" |
| Loot picker, nothing found | Add "{typed}" as a one-off |

## Design tokens (Folio; conventions ruled 2026-10-25)

Every value in code comes from a token in `src/ui/tokens.css`; the gallery test flags raw pixels, sizes and colours. The screens in `docs/design/r1/` still show older values; the tokens win.

**Colours** (light / dark)

| Token | Light | Dark |
|---|---|---|
| Page / Surface / Sheet | #FAFAF7 / #F2F1EC / #FFFFFF | #12100D / #1C1915 / #1E1A16 |
| Ink / Muted | #191714 / #5E574C | #F1EADC / #B5AA96 |
| Rule (passive dividers only) | #D6D3CC | #3A342B |
| **Control border** (anything tappable, ≥3:1) | #8A8378 | #756B5A |
| Accent / text on accent | #6E2A1F / #FFFFFF | Copper #C9875A / #12100D |
| **Selected wash** / its border (every selected state: choice options, segments, chips, tabs; tf-4, 2026-11-03) | #F3E7E3 / Accent #6E2A1F | #3A2A20 / Copper #C9875A |
| Warning (▲) | #7A4A00 | #F2D46B |
| Danger (✕) | #9B1C1C | #F08A7E |
| Info (ⓘ) | #2F5D7C | #8DB4D0 |
| Lit-light glow (its own token; also a lit tab's icon, with `--glow-rest` 2 px, `--glow-peak` 8 px, `--glow-still` 6 px and `--glow-breathe` 2.4 s for its halo) | #B97F1C (was #C98A1E; 3:1 as a graphic on page, surface and sheet, S12, 2026-11-14) | #E6BA66 |
| Press flash and hint · Bar track | #EDEAE3 · #E6E4DE | #4A3A22 · #2A251E |
| Dark-sight fill (party circles) | #D3E0E6 | #2C4452 |
| **Book label** (a stat block's labels, regular weight; cr-c2, 2026-11-12; `--book-label`) | Brick #B5482C (5.35:1 on the sheet) | Copper #C9875A |
| **Book sub-heading** (the book format's sub-headings over an entry's lists, dec-10 S3; `--book-subhead`; the label brick 55% over ink, as drawn) | #6F3221 (9.75:1 on the sheet, 8.62:1 on the surface) | #DBB494 (9.03:1 on the sheet, 8.13:1 on the pop-up) |
| **Book band** (behind a book table's alternate rows, ds-8, dec-10 S3; `--book-band`; ink 7% over the pop-up; body text on it) | #EFEFEF (ink 15.56:1, muted 6.2:1) | #36312A (ink 10.76:1, muted 5.62:1) |

- Five-step ramps for accent, warning, danger and info; a seven-step neutral ramp.
- Every semantic colour carries a shape: ▲ warning, ✕ danger, ⓘ info. No success colour (confirmations are toasts). Destructive actions are secondary buttons with the danger word ("Delete", "Remove"), never filled red (fl-1).
- The gallery tests every contrast pair in both themes.

**Dark mode**
- Every token has a dark value; no hard-coded colours.
- Depth comes from lighter surfaces (page → surface → sheet → pop-up; the dark pop-up surface is #28231D, gallery review 1); outlines carry tappable edges; shadows aren't relied on. Text is never pure white.
- Pictures are never inverted. In dark mode they get a thin control-border frame, and the device switch **"Dim pictures in dark mode"** (⋯ → This device, default On) slightly lowers their brightness until opened full screen.
- Accent fills stay small (one primary per screen).
- The accent colour is also used for a table's title bar, links, the keyed places' numbers and the edge of a person's box; "one accent" stays one accent **button** per screen (mc-20, 2026-11-11).
- On the Scene the accent button is the first action of Waiting for you; else Move on; else Go to sleep (mc-27, 2026-11-11). With no session, Start session stands in Next turn's place and is the page's accent (st-2, 2026-11-14, extending mc-27). Built as `accentOwner` (`src/app/sceneWaiting.tsx`).

**Spacing** (space-1…space-8): **4 · 8 · 12 · 16 · 24 · 32 · 48 · 64**. No other values.

| Use | Step |
|---|---|
| Icon to label; badge to symbol | 4 |
| Inside a control or row; lines within a group | 8 |
| Section padding; between tappable rows | 12 |
| Page gutter (phone and tablet) | 16 |
| Between groups or sections | 24 |
| Between major regions | 32 |
| Screen top/bottom; empty-state room | 48 / 64 |

**Pop-up widths** (fb7-3, fb7-4, 2026-11-20): `--popup-width-min`, **440 px**, a pop-up's card on a tablet; `--popup-width-max`, **720 px**, the Wide pop-up. On the Player tab on a tablet a pop-up fits its content between the two. Never wider than the screen less its margins. Both in `tokens.css`; `.dialog`, `.dialog--wide` and `.dialog--centred` in `Dialog.css` use them (row fb-7, `plans/dec-player-popups-centred.md`). The table sheet (the page's 1024 px), the picture pop-up (1120 px) and the side panel (480 px) keep their own sizes.

**Type** (text-14…text-48). **One named exception under 14** (Francis, 2026-11-25, `design/rulings/dec-save-bonus-in-number-2026-11-24.md`): a Save tile's at-times line is 12 px, by the role token `--text-save-line: calc(12px * var(--text-scale, 1))`, used only in the save tile's CSS (a guard test holds it there). There is no `--text-12`, and nothing else is under 14. Line height 1.5 for body, 1.2 for headings and display numbers. **Tabular lining figures** for any number that changes or aligns. Reading text (rules pop-ups, ⓘ) is capped at about **65 characters** per line on tablet.

| Size | Font | Used for |
|---|---|---|
| 12 (`--text-save-line`) | The tile caption's own | **The one named exception:** a Save tile's at-times line ("11 vs venom"), nothing else. Not a step of the scale |
| 14 | Atkinson | Meta, captions, source lines (minimum) |
| 16 | Atkinson | Body, sheet text, buttons, rows |
| 20 | Garamond | Labels, item names, section headings (small caps) |
| 24 | Garamond | Saves, step titles, dialog titles |
| 30 | Garamond | Screen titles, the character's name |
| 36 | Garamond | AC, attack figure |
| 48 | Garamond | HP, the largest |

Fonts: EB Garamond 600 (names, labels in small caps, display numbers) and Atkinson Hyperlegible 400/700 (body, sub-lines, buttons); both Open Font License, bundled for offline use.

**Bold** `--weight-bold` (700; tk-1, `design/rulings/dec-morning-2026-11-15.md`): a line's emphasis outside the book's type, such as the Roll row's instruction (`dice.css`). Inside the book-type scope it points at the book's own bold, `--weight-book-name` (`creation.css`), as the scope repoints `--weight-heading`. Defined in `src/ui/tokens.css`.

**Book text** (December 5c, type (B) chosen by Francis 2026-11-12; mockup `design/mockups/december/06-stat-block.html`): the book's own words in a stat block, never the app's own text, in their own tokens: **book text** `--font-book-text` (Bitter 400; the name at `--weight-book-name`, 700), **book label** `--font-book-label` (Bitter, at `--weight-book-label`, regular, in `--book-label`), **book descriptor** `--font-book-descriptor` (Alegreya Sans SC 700). Label weight and colour are one token each. Both faces Open Font License, bundled. The source line under a block stays in the body font.

**Creation in the book's type** (dec-10 S2; cs-1, ts-2, ts-3): until the sweep moves the pair to the whole app, character creation alone repoints `--font-body` and `--font-heading` to `--font-book-text` and `--weight-heading` to `--weight-book-name`, by `BookType` (`src/ui/creation.tsx`; `.book-type` in `creation.css`), which `CreationLeave` wraps around every step and whose pop-ups take it through `BookTypeScope` (`Dialog.tsx`); the gallery's creation specimens are drawn in it too. Campaign setup shares the step frame and keeps today's faces. **The faces alone** (po-3, the lead 2026-11-14): `BookFaces` (`src/ui/BookFaces.tsx`; `.book-faces` in `src/ui/bookFaces.css`) repoints the same tokens and draws the small-capitals labels in Alegreya Sans SC, and changes nothing else (creation's row lines, its fold chevron and the book format stay `.book-type`'s); its pop-ups take it through `BookFacesScope` (`Dialog.tsx`). The GM's place page (dec-7 piece 5b) is the first screen in it. Figures take Bitter's plain zero; a lone number box (`.typed-stepper__input`, `.stepper__value`: the score, hit point, coin and age steppers) takes the face's normal figures, as the words beside it do, while figures in columns (tiles, tables) stay aligned (cs-40b).

Shapes: section divider 3px double rule; row divider 1px dotted rule; corners 4px on controls, 20px on the top of sheets.

Taps: 44px minimum with 8px between; HP −/+ 56px; other −/+ 48px; tab bar 62px in Garamond small caps; the light's ring as a tap 72 by 76 px (`--ring-tap-width`, `--ring-tap-height`; lt-2, page 35; a still ring keeps the same 72 px column).

Widths (both scale with the device's text size): `--fact-tile`, a Scene fact tile at its narrowest (216 px, S4); `--place-name-floor`, the width the place page's name column asks for beside the rule's button and Move on (13 rem, 208 px; the lead, 2026-11-14), never wider than its line; names break only between words; a single word wider than its line breaks (the lead, 2026-11-14).

Tap behavior: tappable lines flash on press (≥150ms) then open a details sheet; one-time hint "Tap any line for its rule or details."

## Component patterns (ruled 2026-10-25)

1. **Number inputs open a number pad.** HP changes use the **damage pad** in the GM's views (the Player screen changes them in place with − and +, the HP box, 2026-11-08) (M6-3, ruled 2026-10-26, replacing the quick chips): titled "Damage or heal", the targets named (each removable with ✕), Damage / Heal, and a **stepper −5 −1 [N] +1 +5** starting at that person's last amount (tap the number to type a big one); the button names the result: **"Take N damage"**, **"Deal N to <target>"**, **"Deal N to each"** or **"Heal N"**. No fixed quick amounts: they go stale as characters rise. Only the GM's pad has **Add a target** (area damage).
2. **Undo over confirm** (see principles). The B25 equip dialog is a toast ("Chainmail stops arcane casting · Undo").
3. **Tappable things** have a control border or a filled surface; **static tiles** (stats, saves, combat figures) are tinted with no border.
4. **Never disabled;** blocked actions explain why in a pop-up (B17's "Choose" stays enabled on not-allowed options).
5. **Targets** ≥44px with 8px between. The 8px holds between separate controls (a button and a row, two buttons, a link and a toggle); rows of one list and tiles of one set, each a whole target at least 44px tall, need no gap between them (t44-1).
6. **Every view covers these states:** empty, loading (skeleton rows), partial, overflow (long names, 0 and 999), error, offline (below).
7. **Reduced motion:** rings, bars and skeletons step or hold still instead of animating.
8. **Primary action:** the phone's sticky bottom action bar; top right on tablet (see principles).

## Offline and sync states (ruled 2026-10-25)

- A tappable **"Offline · N waiting"** pill; its details sheet says what works offline, lists your waiting changes (each with Undo) and when the last sync was.
- Your own values not yet synced carry a **"waiting"** tag.
- Other people's live values show an **age label** only when older than 2 minutes.
- Reconnecting shows a **"Catching up…"** pill.
- A change the server **rejects** shows a plain-words toast and a **✕ log entry** that stays.
- **Offline hides nothing:** actions that can't work offline stay visible and explain why on tap.
- **The GM offline** (ruled 2026-10-25): players see an ⓘ pill **"GM offline · time paused"**, cleared by the log line "GM was offline N min · N turns caught up". A second GM device's pill reads **"Read-only · other device running"**, with the full sentence on tap (gallery review 1).
- **Pill shapes** (gallery review 1): "Offline · N waiting" is the ▲ warning pill; Catching up, GM offline and Read-only stay ⓘ info pills. The "waiting" tag is dashed; others' ages read "seen N min ago" (gallery review 2).

## Components (ruled 2026-10-25)

The interface is built from one inventory of parts (atomic design).

1. Every reusable part of the interface is listed once here, with its level, where it's used, and the ruling it came from.
2. **Build once.** A screen composes listed parts; it never rebuilds one. A part needed on a second screen moves into the inventory in the same change.
3. **Change once.** Changing a part changes it everywhere it's used. A screen that needs a variant gets a named variant here, not a copy.
4. Claude Code keeps the **Code** column (the component's name in `src/`) current in the same pull request as any change, and flags any duplicates it finds. "—" means not built yet or not yet audited.
5. Design principles (06) still apply to every part. The inventory says *what* exists; the principles say *how* it behaves.

Levels: **Atoms** (tokens and single controls) → **Molecules** (small groups with one job) → **Organisms** (self-contained sections) → **Templates** (page layouts) → **Pages** (actual screens).

### Atoms

| Part | What it is | Source | Code |
|---|---|---|---|
| Colour tokens | Folio light (Bone) and dark; accent; warning; party circles | 06 tokens | `src/ui/tokens.css` |
| Type scale | Garamond names/labels (small caps), Atkinson body; 14 → 52 px steps | 06 tokens | `src/ui/tokens.css` (`--text-*`), `fonts.css` |
| Spacing and shapes | 18 gutter, 12 section, 8 inner; double section rule, dotted row rule; 4 px / 20 px corners | 06 tokens | `src/ui/tokens.css` (`--space-*`, `--radius-*`) |
| Tap sizes | 44 minimum; 56 HP ±; 48 other ± | 06 tokens | `src/ui/tokens.css`, `base.css` |
| Icon | One family, the same icon for the same thing (placeholders now). The app's icons are medieval or fantasy in nature (ds-5, 2026-11-12), from one open-licence set, game-icons.net (CC BY 3.0, each icon's author credited); nothing under another licence is mixed in, and each icon taken is recorded with its name, author and licence; icons already in the app change at the sweep, the two styles side by side until then (ds-6, 2026-11-12) | 2026-10-23 | `Icon` (`src/ui/icons.tsx`); the step and event symbols (B11) are `StepIcon` in the same file, drawn inline. Variant **filled** (ds-6, dec-9 A): a name from the icon set draws the set's one path on its 512 grid, filled; the set's table, with each icon's author and licence, is `src/ui/iconSet.ts`, its source `.svg` files in `src/ui/iconSet/`; a pack's icon name is read by `packIconOf` (pf-12c); a way of travelling's icon by `wayIconOf` in `src/ui/iconSet.ts` (pf-25; plan scene-point-to-point B16, part 1: the reader only, no button draws it yet) |
| Button row | A choice whose options all fit one row at Standard text size; otherwise a vertical list (B19); none = "—"; variant **small** (the small secondary size, still 44 px; the Magic section's tabs past two rows, Design on #482); variant **two-sided switch** (ds-1d: the sides joined in one frame with no gap between them, an exception to the 8 px between targets since it is one control; the chosen one the selected wash, the accent edge and a bold name, no tick; three or four parts wrap to a second row on a phone; a name table's parts in creation, cs-7, dec-10 D) | M4 gate 7, B19, ds-1d | `ButtonRow` (`src/ui/parts.tsx`, `small`, `sides`: `.button-row--switch` in `parts.css`) |

the buttons are "Lower <label>" / "Raise <label>"): `TypedStepper` (`parts.tsx`, `.typed-stepper`; `describedBy` ties the box to its hint or caption for a screen reader, follow-up E; in the gallery's Creation section; the gallery's `stock-toggle` section; on no screen until TC10 and TC13)), `Chip` (`parts.tsx`); variant **chip with ✕** (something set, by its name, in the Selected wash; the tap takes it off; fl-3): `RemoveChip` (`parts.tsx`, `.chip--remove`); Five across stays on a tablet (80 px tiles at 1024 px, 97 px at 1180 px). **The Saves heading:** the roll line ends with an ⓘ (44 px) that opens the general rule's pop-up (the roll line, the pack's `saveRule`, the source; Player tab pop-ups); at 1024 px the roll line with its ⓘ takes its own row under "Saves", the heading 49 px high (24 without); a pack with a roll line and no `saveRule` has no ⓘ. **The shared line** sits under the heading, above the tiles, once, at 14 px in ink: "Vs magic: each save 1 lower (Spell ward, from Lore)."; only when the rules set gives every save a bonus at times; a line an entry, and its words for two parts or a name that begins "vs" as 05 gives them. **For the build:** a layout check at 80 px with "20 vs venom" in Chromium and WebKit accepts one line or the break "20 vs" / "venom" and nothing else (Chromium runs that one spec file only: `testing.md`, Layout checks); the layout checks' "nothing under 14 px" lets the at-times line's selector through by name | Player screen; the full sheet | ruled 2026-10-26; 2026-11-07; 2026-11-25 | `SaveRow` (`parts.tsx`; `.save-tiles` grid of `StatTile small`); with its breakdown sheet `SaveTiles` (`src/app/FigureSheets.tsx`), used by the Player screen's `Saves` and `SheetView` |

The table's `rollPrompt` (R3-2) is its Roll row's words when the pack gives one. **Long variant (m5b-long-tables, 2026-11-09; mockup creation/final/13, B with C's field; Design's rounds 2 and 3 on #571):** every table sheet, describing and name alike, through one part (`LongRows`, shared by `OneTableSheet` and `NameTableSheet`); the folds of 25 are gone everywhere. Under the Roll row (and a name table's part tabs) a strip sticky at the top of the sheet's scroll, one line each: the **typed roll**, on any table with a die ("What you rolled", a box two taps wide, `calc(var(--tap) * 2)`, `inputmode="numeric"`, and **Show**, the box and Show one group that never wraps (at Large text only the label goes above them); whole numbers only; Show filters to the one row the number falls in, a ranged row included, on a name table that row's cell in the open part, with **Show every row** under it; typing alone changes nothing; it writes nothing), then the **range bar** from **50 rows** (fewer just scroll) (`ButtonRow` "Go to rows", one line that scrolls sideways when it doesn't fit; a tap scrolls to the band, hiding nothing, and empties the box). No helper line in the strip: "Whole numbers only." or "No row has 140." shows under it only when needed (the number and every row kept). The bands go by the number of rows, never the die: an even split into bands of 25–40 rows, each labelled by the die values its rows cover ("1–25" … "76–100" for a d100 with a row per value; "1–50", "51–100" for 50 rows of two) (`rowBands`). The rows: one block per band, two columns on a tablet from **24 rows** (filled down the left first), one on a phone; opened on a pick, the sheet starts at it (`TableSheet`'s `long`; `.table-sheet__bar`, `.table-typed`, `.table-sheet__long--columns` in `creation.css`; `typedRow`, `rowBands` in `kindredTables.ts`). A table with no die and under 24 rows: the plain list. **In creation** (cs-7, dec-10 D, picture 19-D2) its rows are the book's tapped table, whether drawn as Option rows (the name table) or Pick rows (Looks, the trinket, trait lists, a long table's cells): one block ruled between rows and banded (`--book-band`), the picked row the selected wash, the accent edge, bold and its tick, each row still a 44 px tap; a name table adds the part's title bar and a head row naming the die (`.book-tap-head`, sharing a book table's caption and head rules; `NameTableSheet`, through `LongRows`' `head`, after the typed-roll strip), its parts the two-sided switch. |

**Duplicates found (audit 2026-10-26), resolved 2026-10-26:**
- Rules for this campaign is one part: the screen (`RulesForCampaign`, `src/app`) draws the gallery's organism (`RulesForThisCampaign`, `src/ui/organisms.tsx`).
- Optional rules are chosen only in Rules for this campaign (one group per source since 2026-11-02, lr-7). Setting rules shows them read-only with "Change rules for this campaign"; the separate editor there is gone. `OptionalRules` stays for choosing them while adding setting rules mid-campaign (`SettingRulesAdd`); New campaign uses the shared rows (0d.3).

### Pages

Sign-in · Your campaigns · Player · Full sheet · Pack · Scene (players and GM) · Players (GM) · Rules (GM) · Explore and Downtime (M5) · Fight (M6) · Character creation · Charter, Vote, Treasury (companies). Each page lists its template and organisms in 06.

### States, variants and accessibility

Every part documents, in its 06 row: **states** (default, pressed, empty, hidden from this viewer, loading, error, nothing to show → collapses), **named variants** (never copies), **accessibility** (screen-reader label, shape as well as colour, tap size ≥44), the **source ruling**, and a **code column** Claude Code keeps current in the same pull request as any change.

Default state sets by kind:
- Buttons: default, pressed (drops 1 px, shadow off, press flash), busy. Never disabled: a blocked action stays tappable and explains why in a pop-up.
- Lines and rows: default, pressed (flash ≥150 ms), selected, requirement not met (the note "Needs the GM's special approval"; full contrast, never greyed, still pickable; ruled 2026-11-02), chosen (✓ in the fixed slot plus the heavier border), viewed in list and detail (the heavier border, no ✓), hidden from this viewer (not rendered).
- Panels: content, empty (collapses, space reclaimed between actions only), loading (skeleton rows).
- Pop-ups: toast (information, 6 s with Undo where something left your view), dialog (only for irreversible or high-cost choices).
- Every view: empty, loading skeleton, partial, overflow, error, offline placeholder, reduced motion.

**From the gallery reviews** (reviews 1 and 2, ruled 2026-10-25; absorbed 2026-10-29):

| Part | Rule |
|---|---|
| Secondary button | Label at Garamond 20 (the labels step), so three alignment buttons fit one row at 375 px |
| Fill and chips | Ink fill means "on" only; action chips ("All 6", "−1") are outlined |
| Toast | Ink toast in both themes |
| Skeleton rows | Bars one neutral step lighter than their container (visible in dark) |
| Status symbol | Always drawn with its ring track: a hidden timer is an unfilled track; stacks are the ring plus a badge; a low status keeps its letter, adds a small warning sign opposite the stack badge (the ▲ until 2026-11-07, plan m5b-players-screens-standard A3), and takes a warning-colour ring |
| Damage pad | The amount field has a visible "Amount" label; with no amount entered the button reads "Take damage" / "Heal", and a tap explains "Enter an amount" |
| Stat tiles | Tiles in a row are equal height, labels top-aligned; labels stored lowercase so small caps render the same. Variant **tile label, plain** (dec-13 C15, frame P1; Francis's fix 2 of 19 Nov 2026): on the Player screen the armour class, attack, score and save tiles' labels are whole words in plain case, muted, in the book's text face ("Armour class", "Attack", "Muscle"); the hit points box's label takes the section headings' look instead (variant **hit points heading**, below); under each score its modifier always shows, "+0" for a score that changes nothing, on the full sheet's page too, and the tile is heard with it, as the others are (tfr-3, 2026-11-19, page M1; it reversed fix 3's "none"; built, row C11); on the Player screen a score with a change waiting for the GM keeps its modifier and its tile says nothing of the change (page M1, frame M1g), and so does the full sheet's own page (of-3, 2026-11-20); the Attack tile's figure is signed when the rules read the attack as a bonus added to the d20 ("+1", as P1's "+2"; a target number never); on the Player screen the Attack tile's line is in plain case, a capital on its first word only ("Melee +2, missile +1"; dec-14 row 7b, frame 38-7), and the full sheet's tile keeps a capital on each kind (tfw-7). Code: `.player .book-type :is(.stat-row, .score-row, .save-tiles) .stat-tile__label` in `PlayerScreen.css`; the labels, the signed attack (`attackIsBonus`, `src/modules/characters/play.ts`) and the attack line's plain case (`plainCase`) in `player/PlayerVitals.tsx`; the modifier in `CharacterSheet.tsx` (`modifierOf`) |
| Busy text | "Saving…" with the ellipsis character |
| List and detail | The selected row matches the open panel; on a tablet the primary action sits top right (Resend beside the heading; Choose at the top of the detail panel), and on a phone in the sticky bottom bar |
| Kit card | "N things inside" has a disclosure chevron on a 44 px row; "▲ Over by N gp" sits next to the cost; the card is capped at about 560 px on a tablet |
| Starting money | Over budget reads "Broken rule: over by 3 gp · gold starts at 0 if the GM approves" |
| Rules for this campaign | Every group folded with its count line ("3 settings · 1 changed"; a source's "23 rules · 2 changed"); a changed app setting carries "Changed · default <value>"; on a phone the count wraps under the heading; the source tag looks the same in both themes and the app's own tag reads the app-name constant |
| Figures | Atkinson's slashed zero stays (a deliberate legibility feature) |

### Gallery and screenshot tests

- **Component gallery:** a hidden developer page showing every part in every state, light and dark, at Standard / Large / Larger text, at phone and tablet widths, with invented sample data. Built **first**, right after the M4b gate fixes, before more M5 screens.
- **Gallery tests:** contrast on every token pair in both themes; no text under 14px (but a Save tile's at-times line, 12 px, let through by its selector's name once built; Type); targets ≥44px; no raw spacing, type or colour values; every listed state (empty, loading skeleton, partial, overflow, error, offline placeholder, reduced motion); the words-guide check on app-written strings.
- **Screenshot tests:** an automated check photographs the gallery at phone and tablet widths in both themes on every change and compares it with approved pictures; Francis approves intended changes.
- **One audit:** when the code column is first filled, list parts built twice in M1–M4b and merge them during M5.
